SCOPE: DXF2BIM inside the Modeller: Duplex → standard 2D set → import → metadata editor → qualify → convert (basic shapes) → IFC4; then the demo film.
Spec before code, for every lane below. Read the log after every run (exit code is not evidence). Every value traces to a DXF entity, a marking a user confirmed, or a named standard convention; nothing is guessed. Work in a /tmp/wt-* worktree (editing ~/bim-ootb directly is hook-blocked). Neutral naming: no client names anywhere.

## ▶▶▶ RESUME (written 2026-10-18 evening; READ THIS FIRST, then the L7.x sections it names; the 2026-10-17 block below is history)

### STANDING ORDERS (user, 2026-10-18; they override every habit of asking)
1. **We do not hand-build. We write the code that does it.** Every body of the shed must come from GENERIC code in the product (pre-processor / bodies / importer), driven only by the drawing, the layers the user types and the few facts a person states. Scripts such as `~/Projects/SYSNOVA/DXF2BIM/ShedDrawing/tools/shed_scene.js`, `*_probe.js`, `film_*.js` are ORACLES and probes only: use them to MEASURE parity (counts, sizes, positions) of the generic result, never as the deliverable and never as film footage of "an import".
2. **Iterate through the whole queue below to its end WITHOUT asking permission, "go ahead" or which-next.** Spec first (lane doc), code, witness (each check names the issue it proves, with a falsifier), real-shed run, PR, merge, next item. Stop only for a fact ONLY the owner has: then record it as a yellow default / user-stated value / owner question, and continue with the next item. Never invent a value; never claim "first"/"only".
3. **Abstraction:** logic lives in PURE modules (UMD, Node + browser) that the Modeller UI merely adapts; every rule must be runnable and witnessed in Node without a browser. Today the engine modules are already pure (`modeller/dxf2prep.js`, `dxf2bodies.js`, `dxf2bim_import.js`, verbatim `dxf2bim/*`); the editor's suggestion rules (`propGrid`, `propGridTicks`, `propZGround`, PR-VIEW/NAME/FACE/HAXIS, in `dxf_meta_editor.js`'s closure) and `structureGate()` (`dxf_import_ui.js`) are NOT: Q0 below extracts them.
4. Neutral naming: the shed's client is named in the TITLE BLOCK of every sheet: never in a repo, film frame, doc or filename; sheets and the ShedDrawing folder are local only (private OCI copy: bucket `bim-ootb-backup`, `private/dxf2bim/local_2026-10-18e.tgz`, newest key wins; never the public `bim-ootb` bucket). Film: render the 2D sheets with the client TEXT filtered out (the two TEXT entities on each plan sheet), check frames.
5. Git: worktrees only (`/tmp/wt-*`; editing ~/bim-ootb is hook-blocked). The user made me **git admin**: merge my own PRs when `fast-checks` + `e2e-tests` are green (`gh pr merge N --squash`, BIMCompiler: `--repo red1oon/BIMCompiler`). GitHub may AUTO-MERGE a PR after its first push: after pushing more commits to an open PR check `gh pr view N --json state`; if it already merged, open a NEW PR from the branch (merge `origin/main` in, conflicts in this doc are append-append: keep both). BIMCompiler change -> merge there FIRST, then `modeller/dxf2bim/sync.sh ~/bim-compiler <master commit>`. CI also runs the no-undef gate: run `~/bim-ootb/node_modules/.bin/eslint viewer modeller` (0 errors) BEFORE pushing (a Node `Buffer`/`process` use needs `require('buffer')` etc.). Local only (no CI): `witness_dxf_meta_editor.js`, `witness_dxf_import_node.js` need BIMCompiler sandbox outputs; `sw.js` CACHE_VERSION is bumped at deploy time only.
6. Chapter cards: the user wants the films in the established dual-colour chapter-card format (`~/bim-compiler/prompts/film_title_cards.py`: CHAPTER n kicker coral, title line 1 off-white, line 2 coral, one plain line, dimmed still backdrop, persistent chapter tag + series tag) and the real 2D drawings VISIBLE. See Q1.

### THE QUEUE (work in this order; one lane-doc spec section + witnesses + real-shed parity + PR per item)
**Q0 Abstraction (do first).** New pure modules: `modeller/dxf2suggest.js` (all PR-* rules: `proposalsFor(sheet, ctx)` with ctx = the other sheets, unit/layer-set maps; no `window`, no `S`), `modeller/dxf2structure.js` (the structure gate: roles, mixed-set refusal, `Dxf2Bodies.buildBodies`, verdict/by_shape/R shape). `dxf_meta_editor.js` and `dxf_import_ui.js` become thin adapters (same behaviour: W-DXF-ATTN 17, -STRUCT 15, -TICKS 10, -SHED 11 must stay green). A headless runner `modeller/dxf2bim_cli.js`: `node dxf2bim_cli.js --sheets a.dxf b.dxf ... --accept-suggestions --set SHED --level 0 --datum <sheet>:0.6096@<y> --layer plan:Col=column --layer elev:0=column --bodyz footing=-1.4478,-1.143 --out shed.ifc` writing the marked DXFs, the verdict and (via `Bonsai.ifc` equivalent or `ifc_extract_core`) the IFC/DB; witness `witness_dxf_cli.js` = the real-shed result equals the browser witness (32 + 32, same z, same ids). Spec it as lane §L8.
**Q1 Film v2.** (a) Generalise `film_title_cards.py` to N chapters (`len(chap) != len(CARDS)` instead of 6; add `FILM_SET=bricscad`: 4 chapters OPEN / MEANING / BODIES / AN ORDER; BIMCompiler PR). (b) Recorder emits `§FILM_CHAPTER n=.. key=.. t=..` + `§FILM_BEAT` and a backdrop still per chapter; (c) the real 2D sheets VISIBLE: a beat showing the rendered sheets (above), and `DxfImportUI.setLines(true)` so the sheets sit in the 3D canvas where they belong once they have roles (verify no client text); (d) assemble script = `film_dxf2bim_assemble.sh` + the cards step (see `film_title_cards.py` header for the pipeline); (e) frame QC automated (OCR-free: the filtered sheets never contain the strings; assert on the DXF entities used). Then the Malay track (`film_narration_poly.py`, lang `ms`).
**Q2 Beams and grade beams (generic).** Drawn as two parallel lines (12 in apart) broken at every column and in different pieces on the two edges, ALSO in the elevation as a horizontal band (soffit pieces + a long top line), and the expansion-joint lines look the same (8.7 in). Spec: band regions in BOTH orientations (pair rule per axis, region = overlap of the two edges, per-span bodies between column faces: NO bridging); the joint must not become a beam: add a per-region user EXCLUDE for bodies (editor row + marking, grammar via BIMCompiler) and report excluded regions; heights from elevation bands (horizontal pair, z from the datum), else `@BODYZ`; multi-plan beam layout aligned by grid labels (done). Parity oracle: `shed_scene.js` beams (B-1 12"x10" left block, B-2 12"x12" right block): NOTE the South elevation draws BOTH blocks 10 in deep (z 122..132): a drawing conflict with the B-2 label: surface it (advisory), do not reconcile.
**Q3 Gable-end walls.** HATCH boundary loops (layer `Wall`, pattern BLOCKS) on the West/East elevations: one entity can hold several loops (ids `-L<i>`, done in the script, port it): thickness from the label (`10" Brick Wall`/`5" Brick Wall`, leader tip inside a panel) as a yellow default; the fan openings are the gaps in the outline. Oracle: `shed_scene.js` gable section (12 bodies, volume = area x thickness to 1e-13).
**Q4 Roof.** Sloped panels from the truss section (`8_SECTION_AA_TRUSS.dxf`: 17.5 deg, ridge on the centre line, roofing 4.2 in above the top chord, 18 in overhang, ridge mark on the beam plan) + the building footprint; sheet thickness = yellow default (1 in display). Needs a `roof` body kind + the roof-plan role question (the structure gate has no roof plan: the roof is a body from the section, not a roof plan).
**Q5 Trusses / purlins / gussets.** A TYPICAL section (sheet 8, layer `Fo`: chords, king post, verticals, diagonals, purlin angles, gusset quads) repeated along the length: the spacing is NOT drawn (one section line on the plan, at no column line): default every column line, RED (assumed) + yellow default `truss.spacing` with options (every column line / every 2nd) + owner question; member sizes from labels (`2 - 2"x2"x1/4"`, `1.5"x1.5"x1/4" Angle`: square envelope assumed: yellow default). Oracle: the 318 truss bodies of `shed_scene.js`.
**Q6 Doors, fans, EC pads, zigzag hatch.** Doors: the drawn gaps in the support walls (3, plan text "Door"; heights not drawn: yellow default); fans: CIRCLEs on the elevations (13 in plan, 10 in the west elevation; thickness not drawn); EC pads: plan outline + detail section; the East-end ZIGZAG hatch above the low middle bay (net or door?): owner question, build nothing for it.
**Owner questions (carry as yellow defaults / advisories, never block):** roof sheet thickness; truss spacing and whether the right block repeats the left; support-wall height (24 in text vs the brick panels 10"/5"); the 2 in between 120 (dimension) and 122 (elevation/section lines); wall centring on the grid line; GB-1 top at FGL or PL; the zigzag hatch.
